a1:T518,type Money: Add, Neg, Comparable {
    cents: Int

    fn add(self, other: Money) -> Money { Money(cents: self.cents + other.cents) }
    fn neg(self) -> Money { Money(cents: -self.cents) }
    fn compare(self, other: Money) -> Int { self.cents - other.cents }
}

// Adds every item to `zero`: T needs only +.
fn total<T: Add>(items: [T], zero: T) -> T {
    let sum = zero
    for x in items {
        sum = sum + x
    }
    sum
}

// Turns x negative: T needs only unary -.
fn flip<T: Neg>(x: T) -> T {
    -x
}

// The item that comes last in order, or none for an empty list: T needs only >.
fn largest<T: Comparable>(items: [T]) -> T? {
    let best = items.get(0)
    for x in items {
        if let b = best {
            if x > b {
                best = x
            }
        }
    }
    best
}

fn main() {
    print(total([1, 2, 3], 0))
    const halves: [Float32] = [0.5, 0.25]
    print(total(halves, Float32(0.0)))
    print(total([Money(cents: 150), Money(cents: 99)], Money(cents: 0)))
    print(flip(5))
    print(flip(Money(cents: 7)))
    print(largest([3, 9, 2]))
    print(largest(["pear", "apple", "zebra"]))
    print(largest([Money(cents: 5), Money(cents: 50)]))
    print(largest([1.5, 0.5]))
    const none_here: [Int] = []
    print(largest(none_here))
    print(7.add(2))

<span class="line"><span style="color:#857C6E">// Adds every item to `zero`: T needs only +.</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#E6A23C">fn</span><span style="color:#8FC1EA"> total</span><span style="color:#E7E0D4">&#x3C;</span><span style="color:#8FC1EA">T</span><span style="color:#E7E0D4">: </span><span style="color:#8FC1EA">Add</span><span style="color:#E7E0D4">>(items: [</span><span style="color:#8FC1EA">T</span><span style="color:#E7E0D4">], zero: </span><span style="color:#8FC1EA">T</span><span style="color:#E7E0D4">) </span><span style="color:#E6A23C">-></span><span style="color:#8FC1EA"> T</span><span style="color:#E7E0D4"> {</span></span>
